I'll now produce final answer.</think>We can notice that if we add 13 and 3 we get 16. Then multiplying 16 by 6 gives 96, and finally dividing 96 by 4 yields 24. In other words,

  (13 + 3) × 6 ÷ 4 = 24.

Thus, the valid expression is

  \boxed{((13+3)\cdot6)/4}.
